```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/spot.py
"""
Canonical SPOT ingest fixture.

11 rows / 11 unique uids. Exercises every resolution path the SPOT
compiler can take against the canonical static + proxy config universe:

Per-row coverage
----------------
  uid  underlying              kind                       notes
  ---  ------------------      -----------------------    ----------------------
   1   AAPL_EQ                 node, no FX                long
   2   AAPL_EQ                 node, no FX                long  (duplicate row
                                                                of uid 1 — same
                                                                underlying,
                                                                identical
                                                                resolution path)
   3   USDEUR                  node, FX direct hit        long
   4   USDEUR                  node, FX direct hit        short (settle ccy
                                                                forces inversion)
   5   USDGBP                  node, RF-LEVEL PROXY       proxies to USDEUR
                                                          scale 0.85
   6   EURCHF                  node, RF-LEVEL PROXY       proxies to USDEUR
                                                          scale 0.60
   7   BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]    curve-point, direct        long
   8   BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]    curve-point, direct        short, EUR settle
                                                          (exercises FX leg)
   9   WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]      curve-point, CONTAINER     proxies to
                               PROXY                      BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]
                                                          scale 0.95
  10   HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD[M+1]  curve-point, CONTAINER     proxies to
                               PROXY                      BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+1]
                                                          scale 1.40
  11   USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y]       curve-point, RF-LEVEL      proxies to
                               PROXY                      USD_SOFR_OIS[10Y]
                                                          scale 1.00

Netting-set linkage is exercised across uids 1-4 (three sets) so the
credit-exposure pipeline has something to chew on without forcing the
SPOT validator into any new branches.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import (
    generate_netting_set_ids,
    validate_column_lengths,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import InstrumentType

_UIDS: list[int] = list(range(1, 12))  # uids 1..11, contiguous and unique.
_NUM_UIDS: int = len(_UIDS)


def _get_data() -> MarketRiskIngestData:
    return {
        "uid": _UIDS,
        "instrument": [InstrumentType.SPOT.name] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "quantity": [
            100.0,  # uid 1.  long AAPL
            100.0,  # uid 2.  long AAPL (duplicate underlying)
            5_000_000.0,  # uid 3.  long USDEUR FX spot
            -5_000_000.0,  # uid 4.  short USDEUR (settle USD forces inversion)
            -3_000_000.0,  # uid 5.  short USDGBP (RF-proxy underlying)
            2_000_000.0,  # uid 6.  long  EURCHF (RF-proxy underlying)
            10_000.0,  # uid 7.  long  BRENT front-month
            -5_000.0,  # uid 8.  short BRENT front-month in EUR settle
            8_000.0,  # uid 9.  long  WTI  front-month (container-proxy)
            -2_500.0,  # uid 10. short HENRY_HUB [M+1] (container-proxy)
            1_000_000.0,  # uid 11. long  USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y] (RF-proxy)
        ],
        "underlying": [
            "AAPL_EQ",  # node, no FX
            "AAPL_EQ",  # node, no FX (dup)
            "USDEUR",  # node, direct FX
            "USDEUR",  # node, inverse direct FX
            "USDGBP",  # node, RF-proxy
            "EURCHF",  # node, RF-proxy
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",  # curve-point, direct
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",  # curve-point, direct (different settle)
            "WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",  # curve-point, container-proxy
            "HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD[M+1]",  # curve-point, container-proxy
            "USD_LIBOR_3M[10Y]",  # curve-point, RF-proxy
        ],
        "settlement_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "EUR",
            "USD",
            "GBP",
            "CHF",
            "USD",
            "EUR",  # forces FX leg over the BRENT[M+0] underlying
            "USD",
            "USD",
            "USD",
        ],
        # netting_set_id: small set used by exposure pipeline. None -> no
        # netting linkage.
        "netting_set_id": generate_netting_set_ids(n=_NUM_UIDS, none_probability=0.5),
        # SPOT-irrelevant columns: all None.
        "strike_price": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "strike_ccy": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "is_call": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "expiry_date": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "delivery_date": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "settlement_date": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "under_discount_curve": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "strike_discount_curve": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
        "volatility_container_name": [None] * _NUM_UIDS,
    }


CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA: MarketRiskIngestData = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA)


_CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF = pd.DataFrame(
    CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA
)


def _debug_print() -> None:

        print_df,

    print_calling_file()

    print("As DataFrame:")
    print_df(_CANONICAL_SPOT_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF)

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/vanilla_european_option_curve.py
"""
Canonical VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE ingest fixture.

VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE = European option on a forward curve.
Underlying is a curve container; vol is sourced from a surface container;
delivery + expiry + strike + is_call + vol-container all live.

Coverage:
  - Call on commodity curve, no FX             -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  vol surface
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF (uid=400)
  - Put  on commodity curve, no FX             -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF (uid=401)
  - Call on commodity curve, FX settlement     -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  EUR settlement
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF (uid=402)
  - Call on commodity curve, CONTAINER PROXY   -> WTI_CRUDE_FWD ->
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_FWD, scale 0.95;
                                                  WTI vol surface is itself
                                                  resolved via the underlying-
                                                  container proxy on the vol
                                                  side at the runtime builder.
                                                  Here we point at
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF
                                                  directly. (uid=403)
  - Put  on FX curve via surface (uid=404)     -> USDEUR; vol surface
                                                  USDEUR_VOL_SURF is
                                                  declared DELTA_CALL — the
                                                  runtime market-data builder
                                                  currently rejects DELTA
                                                  surfaces, so this trade
                                                  compiles cleanly but cannot
                                                  yet be priced end-to-end.
                                                  See notes/REFACTORS_NEEDED.md.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.rng import get_canonical_rng
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import (
    generate_netting_set_ids,
    validate_column_lengths,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import InstrumentType

_rng: np.random.Generator = get_canonical_rng()


def _get_data() -> MarketRiskIngestData:
    return {
        "uid": [400, 401, 402, 403, 404],
        "instrument": [InstrumentType.VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE.name] * 5,
        "quantity": [
            2_000.0,  # 400. long call on Brent fwd
            -1_500.0,  # 401. short put on Brent fwd
            1_000.0,  # 402. long call on Brent fwd, EUR settle
            3_000.0,  # 403. long call on WTI fwd (container-proxy)
            -500.0,  # 404. short put on USDEUR fwd (vol surface USDEUR_VOL_SURF)
        ],
        "underlying": [
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD",
            "WTI_CRUDE_FWD",  # container-proxy
            "USDEUR",
        ],
        "settlement_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
        ],
        "strike_price": [80.0, 75.0, 82.0, 73.0, 1.0900],
        "strike_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
        ],
        "is_call": [1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0],
        "expiry_date": [
            "2026-06-25",
            "2026-09-24",
            "2026-12-24",
            "2026-06-25",
            "2026-09-24",
        ],
        "delivery_date": [
            "2026-07-01",
            "2026-10-01",
            "2027-01-01",
            "2026-07-01",
            None,  # FX option has no underlying delivery
        ],
        "settlement_date": [
            "2026-06-29",
            "2026-09-28",
            "2026-12-28",
            "2026-06-29",
            "2026-09-28",
        ],
        "under_discount_curve": [
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
        ],
        "strike_discount_curve": [
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "EUR_ESTR_OIS",  # cross-curve discount
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "EUR_ESTR_OIS",
        ],
        # Vol-container resolution lands at the ingest perimeter now: the
        # compile step maps each name to an int32 container id via
        # `static_config.container_name_to_id` and derives the int8 y-axis
        # type via `surface_meta_dict[id].y_axis_kind`. uid=404 references
        # a DELTA_CALL surface (USDEUR_VOL_SURF) — that ingests fine but
        # the runtime builder rejects DELTA surfaces today (see
        # notes/REFACTORS_NEEDED.md).
        "volatility_container_name": [
            "BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_VOL_SURF",  # WTI vol surface not in canonical config
            "USDEUR_VOL_SURF",
        ],
        "netting_set_id": generate_netting_set_ids(n=5, none_probability=0.0),
    }


CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA: MarketRiskIngestData = _get_data()

validate_column_lengths(
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_CURVE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/vanilla_european_option_node.py
"""
Canonical VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE ingest fixture.

VANILLA_EUROPEAN_OPTION_NODE = European option whose underlying resolves
to a single risk-factor node (or curve-point relative name) AND whose
implied volatility is sourced from a per-underlying ATM-vol node.

Per the option-NODE vol-source refactor, the canonical static config
now declares scalar ATM-vol nodes (`AAPL_ATM_VOL`, `USDEUR_ATM_VOL`,
`BRENT_CRUDE_ATM_VOL`) alongside the FX / equity / commodity price
nodes. The kernel's `vol_nodes[v_uix]` access pattern lands directly on
those node-typed risk factors — no surface ATM-slice synthesis, no
universe-vs-surface-id slot mismatch.

Coverage:
  - Call on equity node, no FX                 -> AAPL_EQ      (uid=300)
                                                  vol AAPL_ATM_VOL
  - Put  on equity node, no FX                 -> AAPL_EQ      (uid=301)
                                                  vol AAPL_ATM_VOL
  - Call on FX node, settlement in foreign ccy -> USDEUR       (uid=302)
                                                  strike=EUR, settle=EUR
                                                  vol USDEUR_ATM_VOL
  - Put  on FX node, RF-LEVEL PROXY underlying -> USDGBP       (uid=303)
                                                  proxies to USDEUR
                                                  vol USDEUR_ATM_VOL
  - Call on curve-point pinned underlying      -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]
                                                  (uid=304)
                                                  vol BRENT_CRUDE_ATM_VOL
  - Put  on curve-point, CONTAINER PROXY       -> WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]
                                                  proxies to BRENT[M+0]
                                                  (uid=305)
                                                  vol BRENT_CRUDE_ATM_VOL

            -250_000.0,  # 303. short put USDGBP (RF-proxy)
            1_000.0,  # 304. long call BRENT[M+0]
            -500.0,  # 305. short put WTI[M+0] (container-proxy)

        "underlying": [
            "AAPL_EQ",
            "AAPL_EQ",
            "USDEUR",
            "USDGBP",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",
            "WTI_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",
        ],
        "settlement_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "GBP",
            "USD",
            "USD",
        ],
        "strike_price": [180.0, 175.0, 1.0850, 1.2750, 78.0, 75.0],

            "USD",

            "EUR",
            "GBP",
            "USD",
            "USD",
        ],

            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
```

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\ingest_fixtures\portfolio\vanilla_forward_curve.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/vanilla_forward_curve.py
"""
Canonical VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE ingest fixture.

VANILLA_FORWARD_CURVE = forward whose underlying is a *curve container*
(not a single rf). Delivery date selects the curve point at runtime via
the curve's roll rule. Strike + delivery + settlement + discounting are
all live.

Coverage:
  - Curve underlying, no FX, no discount       -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  (uid=200)
  - Curve underlying, no FX, with discount     -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  + USD_SOFR_OIS
                                                  (uid=201)
  - Curve underlying, FX, cross-curve discount -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  EUR settlement,
                                                  USD_SOFR_OIS / EUR_ESTR_OIS
                                                  (uid=202)
  - Curve underlying, CONTAINER PROXY          -> WTI_CRUDE_FWD ->
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_FWD,
                                                  scale 0.95
                                                  (uid=203)
  - Curve underlying, CONTAINER PROXY + FX     -> HENRY_HUB_GAS_FWD ->
                                                  BRENT_CRUDE_FWD,
                                                  EUR settlement,
                                                  scale 1.40 (uid=204)
  - Curve underlying, IBOR discount-basis path -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD
                                                  discounted via
                                                  USD_LIBOR_3M instead
                                                  of OIS — exercises the
                                                  non-default discount
                                                  basis (uid=205)

Not covered here (registered proxy that is not exercisable today):
  POWER_CH_BASELOAD_HY -> CO2_EUA_ANNUAL_FWD
    Source curve is OFFSET_HY, proxy is OFFSET_Y. The resolver's
    container-proxy path requires matching x_axis_kinds across required
    and proxy (PrettyKeyError otherwise). Resolving x_axis_kind mismatch
    is a separate work item; until then this proxy is registered but
    not consumed at ingest.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import (
    generate_netting_set_ids,
    validate_column_lengths,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import InstrumentType


def _get_data() -> MarketRiskIngestData:
    return {
        "uid": [200, 201, 202, 203, 204, 205],
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/portfolio/vanilla_forward_node.py
"""
Canonical VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE ingest fixture.

VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE = forward contract whose underlying resolves to a
single risk-factor node (or a single curve-point relative name). No
vol. Strike, delivery, settlement, and optional cross-curve discounting.

Coverage:
  - Node underlying, no FX, no discount         -> AAPL_EQ      (uid=100)
  - Node underlying, FX, no discount            -> USDEUR       (uid=101)
  - Node underlying, no FX, settlement date     -> AAPL_EQ      (uid=102)
                                                   discount via USD_SOFR_OIS
  - Node underlying, FX, discount + cross-ccy   -> USDEUR       (uid=103)
                                                   strike in EUR, under in USD;
                                                   under_df=USD_SOFR_OIS,
                                                   strike_df=EUR_ESTR_OIS
  - Curve-point underlying (pin to M+0 tenor)   -> BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]
                                                   (uid=104, no FX)
  - Curve-point underlying, RF-LEVEL PROXY      -> USDGBP -> USDEUR
                                                   (uid=105, scale 0.85)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import MarketRiskIngestData
from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.utils import (
    generate_netting_set_ids,
    validate_column_lengths,
)
from riskquant.portfolio.instruments import InstrumentType


def _get_data() -> MarketRiskIngestData:
    return {
        "uid": [100, 101, 102, 103, 104, 105],
        "instrument": [InstrumentType.VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE.name] * 6,
        "quantity": [
            10.0,  # 100. long AAPL fwd
            1_000_000.0,  # 101. long USDEUR fwd
            -5.0,  # 102. short AAPL fwd settle
            500_000.0,  # 103. long USDEUR fwd, EUR strike, discounted
            1_000.0,  # 104. long BRENT[M+0] fwd
            -750_000.0,  # 105. short USDGBP fwd (RF-proxy)
        ],
        "underlying": [
            "AAPL_EQ",
            "USDEUR",
            "AAPL_EQ",
            "USDEUR",
            "BRENT_CRUDE_FWD[M+0]",
            "USDGBP",
        ],
        "settlement_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "USD",
            "EUR",  # strike_ccy=EUR forces cross-curve discounting below
            "USD",
            "GBP",  # forces FX leg + RF-proxy on USDGBP underlying
        ],
        "strike_price": [180.0, 1.0850, 175.0, 1.0900, 78.50, 1.2750],
        "strike_ccy": [
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "USD",
            "EUR",
            "USD",
            "GBP",
        ],
        "delivery_date": [
            None,  # 100. no delivery (node fwd, no physical schedule)
            None,  # 101. no delivery
            None,  # 102. no delivery
            None,  # 103. no delivery
            None,  # 104. delivery semantics live on the curve via roll rule
            None,  # 105. no delivery
        ],
        "settlement_date": [
            None,  # 100. spot-style (no settlement schedule needed)
            None,  # 101.
            "2026-12-15",  # 102. EXERCISES SETTLEMENT DATE
            "2026-12-15",  # 103. EXERCISES SETTLEMENT DATE + cross-curve discount
            None,  # 104.
            "2026-09-30",  # 105. EXERCISES SETTLEMENT + RF-proxy
        ],
        "under_discount_curve": [
            None,
            None,
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            None,
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",  # under is USD-quoted FX rate
        ],
        "strike_discount_curve": [
            None,
            None,
            "USD_SOFR_OIS",
            "EUR_ESTR_OIS",  # cross-curve discount
            None,
            "GBP_SONIA_OIS",
        ],
        # FWD has no expiry / is_call / vol.
        "is_call": [None] * 6,
        "expiry_date": [None] * 6,
        "volatility_container_name": [None] * 6,
        # netting linkage: leave empty here; spot fixture carries it.
        "netting_set_id": generate_netting_set_ids(n=6, none_probability=0.0),
    }


CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA: MarketRiskIngestData = (
    _get_data()
)

validate_column_lengths(CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA)


_CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF = pd.DataFrame(
    CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA
)


def _debug_print() -> None:
    from riskquant.utils.printer import (
        print_calling_file,
        print_df,
        print_dict,
        print_success_message,
    )

    print_calling_file()

    print("Canonical VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE Market-Risk Ingest Data:")
    print_dict(CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DATA)

    print("As DataFrame:")
    print_df(_CANONICAL_VANILLA_FORWARD_NODE_MARKET_RISK_PORTFOLIO_DF)

    print_success_message(add_space_before=False)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _debug_print()
```

```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/rng.py
import numpy as np

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.constants import CANONICAL_RNG_SEED


def get_canonical_rng() -> np.random.Generator:
    """Get a new RNG seeded with the canonical seed."""
    return np.random.default_rng(seed=CANONICAL_RNG_SEED)
```

<file path="riskquant\src\riskquant\ingest_fixtures\schemas.py">
```python
# src/riskquant/ingest_fixtures/schemas.py
"""
Canonical TypedDict schemas for every human-facing ingest frame.

Schemas mirror the SoA contracts they feed:
- Market-risk frames carry trade-keyed columns + optional credit linkage.
- JTD frames carry obligor-keyed *model-specific* columns ONLY. PD and
  LGD live on the obligor master; they are joined onto JTD rows at
  compile time and are never duplicated on per-model frames.
- Obligor- and netting-set-master frames are first-class schemas, one
  per master.
"""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from typing import TypedDict

######################
# RISK STATIC CONFIG #
######################


# ============================================================
# CURRENCY
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigCcy(TypedDict):
    ccy_name: Sequence[str]
    ccy_id: Sequence[int]


# ============================================================
# HOLIDAY CALENDAR
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigHolidayCalendar(TypedDict):
    holiday_calendar_name: Sequence[str]
    holiday_date: Sequence[str]  # ISO yyyy-mm-dd; compiler converts to int32 ordinals


# ============================================================
# SPOT ROLL RULE
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigSpotRollRule(TypedDict):
    rule_name: Sequence[str]
    roll_method: Sequence[str]
    offset_in_tenor_units: Sequence[int]
    roll_month: Sequence[int | None]
    roll_day: Sequence[int | None]
    fh_bh_mmdd: Sequence[str | None]
    post_roll_shift: Sequence[int]


# ============================================================
# NODE  (scalar; IS its own risk factor)
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigNodeMeta(TypedDict):
    node_id: Sequence[int]
    node_name: Sequence[str]
    quote_ccy: Sequence[str]
    asset_class: Sequence[str]
    asset_subclass: Sequence[str]
    quote_type: Sequence[str]
    unit_scale: Sequence[str]
    shift_type: Sequence[str]
    fx_base_ccy: Sequence[str | None]


# ============================================================
# CURVE
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigCurveMeta(TypedDict):
    curve_id: Sequence[int]
    curve_name: Sequence[str]
    quote_ccy: Sequence[str]
    asset_class: Sequence[str]
    asset_subclass: Sequence[str]
    quote_type: Sequence[str]
    unit_scale: Sequence[str]
    shift_type: Sequence[str]
    x_axis_kind: Sequence[str]
    x_interp_method: Sequence[str]
    x_extrap_method: Sequence[str]
    spot_roll_rule_name: Sequence[str | None]
    discount_basis: Sequence[str]
    is_default_discount_for_ccy: Sequence[bool]


class RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta(TypedDict):
    rf_id: Sequence[int]
    curve_id: Sequence[int]
    tenor_token: Sequence[str]


# ============================================================
# SURFACE
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta(TypedDict):
    surface_id: Sequence[int]
    surface_name: Sequence[str]
    quote_ccy: Sequence[str]
    asset_class: Sequence[str]
    asset_subclass: Sequence[str]
    quote_type: Sequence[str]
    unit_scale: Sequence[str]
    shift_type: Sequence[str]
    x_axis_kind: Sequence[str]
    x_interp_method: Sequence[str]
    x_extrap_method: Sequence[str]
    spot_roll_rule_name: Sequence[str | None]
    y_axis_kind: Sequence[str]
    y_interp_method: Sequence[str]
    y_extrap_method: Sequence[str]


class RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta(TypedDict):
    rf_id: Sequence[int]
    surface_id: Sequence[int]
    x_tenor_token: Sequence[str]
    y_label: Sequence[str]


# ============================================================
# TOP-LEVEL
# ============================================================


class RiskStaticConfigData(TypedDict):
    ccy: RiskStaticConfigCcy
    holiday_calendars: RiskStaticConfigHolidayCalendar
    spot_roll_rule: RiskStaticConfigSpotRollRule
    node_meta: RiskStaticConfigNodeMeta
    curve_meta: RiskStaticConfigCurveMeta
    curve_point_meta: RiskStaticConfigCurvePointMeta
    surface_meta: RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta
    surface_point_meta: RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta


#####################################
# END OF RISK STATIC CONFIG SCHEMAS #
#####################################

#####################
# RISK PROXY CONFIG #
#####################


class RiskProxyConfigData(TypedDict):
    proxying_level: Sequence[str]  # "CONTAINER" | "RISK_FACTOR"
    required_name: Sequence[str]
    proxy_name: Sequence[str]
    vol_scaling_factor: Sequence[float]


# ============================================================
# BASE
# ============================================================


class _BaseIngestData(TypedDict):
    uid: Sequence[int]
    instrument: Sequence[str]


# ============================================================
# MARKET RISK
# ============================================================


class _OptionalNettingSet(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Effective-netting-set linkage. Optional: absent/None -> NO_NETTING_SET.
    netting_set_id: Sequence[int | None]


class _SpotForwardOptionFields(TypedDict, total=False):
    # --- SPOT / FORWARD / OPTION ---

    quantity: Sequence[float | None]
    underlying: Sequence[str | None]
    settlement_ccy: Sequence[str | None]

    strike_price: Sequence[float | None]
    strike_ccy: Sequence[str | None]

    is_call: Sequence[bool | None]

    expiry_date: Sequence[str | None]
    delivery_date: Sequence[str | None]
    settlement_date: Sequence[str | None]

    under_discount_curve: Sequence[str | None]
    strike_discount_curve: Sequence[str | None]

    # Name of the volatility container (vol surface for option-curve;
    # vol surface or vol-node container for option-node) the option's
    # implied vol is sourced from. Required for OPTION-family rows,
    # None for SPOT and FORWARD rows. Resolved to an int32 container
    # id at compile time against `static_config.container_name_to_id`.
    volatility_container_name: Sequence[str | None]


class _VanillaIrsFields(TypedDict, total=False):
    notional: Sequence[float | None]

    pay_receive_fixed: Sequence[str | None]

    fixed_rate: Sequence[float | None]
    float_spread: Sequence[float | None]

    settlement_ccy: Sequence[str | None]

    effective_date: Sequence[str | None]
    maturity_date: Sequence[str | None]

    fixed_freq: Sequence[str | None]
    float_freq: Sequence[str | None]

    fixed_daycount: Sequence[str | None]
    float_daycount: Sequence[str | None]

    forecast_curve: Sequence[str | None]
    discount_curve: Sequence[str | None]

    fixed_holiday_calendar: Sequence[str | None]
    float_holiday_calendar: Sequence[str | None]

    fixed_business_day_convention: Sequence[str | None]
    float_business_day_convention: Sequence[str | None]

    date_generation_rule: Sequence[str | None]  # "FORWARD" or "BACKWARD"

    payment_lag: Sequence[int | None]
    fixing_lag: Sequence[int | None]


class MarketRiskIngestData(
    _BaseIngestData,
    _OptionalNettingSet,
    _SpotForwardOptionFields,
    _VanillaIrsFields,
):
    """
    Canonical market-risk fixture schema.

    Represents the union of all supported market-risk instrument
    columns. Each instrument family populates only the subset of
    columns it requires; all other columns are None.

    This schema exists for fixture authoring and validation only.
    It is NOT a runtime contract.
    """

    pass


# ============================================================
# CREDIT MASTERS
# ============================================================


class _OptionalObligorName(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Effective-netting-set linkage. Optional: absent/None -> NO_NETTING_SET.
    name: Sequence[str | None]


class ObligorCreditIngestData(_OptionalObligorName):
    """
    Obligor master ingest frame.

    Single source of truth for obligor-level credit attributes. PD and
    LGD live here, not on per-model JTD frames — that's the duplication
    the master is designed to prevent.

    `parent_uid` is nullable: NA/None marks an ultimate obligor and is
    mapped to `NO_PARENT_OBLIGOR` at compile time. The rating/sector/
    country int codes are placeholders for IntEnum taxonomies wired in a
    follow-up; `NO_RATING_ID`, `NO_SECTOR_ID`, `NO_COUNTRY_ID` mark
    unknowns.
    """

    uid: Sequence[int]
    pd: Sequence[float]
    lgd: Sequence[float]

    rating_id: Sequence[int]  # int8 enum, source: config/enums/credit_ratings (TBD)
    sector_id: Sequence[int]  # int32 enum (TBD)
    country_id: Sequence[int]  # int32 enum (TBD)

    parent_uid: Sequence[int | None]  # None -> NO_PARENT_OBLIGOR


class NettingSetMasterIngestData(TypedDict):
    """
    Netting-set master ingest frame.

    Carries the netting-set identifier (joined to the portfolio's
    `netting_set_id` column), the owning obligor, and net collateral
    (haircut-adjusted, >= 0).
    """

    uid: Sequence[int]
    obligor_uid: Sequence[int]
    net_collateral: Sequence[float]


class _OptionalFactorNu(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Chi degrees of freedom. Present (and > 0) only for CHI rows; absent /
    # None for every other kind, mapped to NO_CHI_NU at compile time.
    nu: Sequence[float | None]


class _OptionalFactorBetaParams(TypedDict, total=False):
    # Beta distribution parameters for Beta PD factors. Present and > 0 only for
    # BETA_PD rows; absent / None for every other kind, mapped to NO_BETA_PARAMS
    # at compile time.
    alpha: Sequence[float | None]
    beta: Sequence[float | None]


class CreditFactorUniverseIngestData(_OptionalFactorNu, _OptionalFactorBetaParams):
    """
    Credit factor-universe master ingest frame.

    Single source of truth for the credit factor space. One row per factor:
    a stable `factor_id` identity (sorted ascending -> dense UIX = row position
    in the (F, S) CreditFactorMatrix), the human `factor_name` obligor JTD
    frames reference, and the `factor_kind` (a `CreditFactorKind` name). `nu`
    is the only kind-specific column: required and > 0 for CHI factors, omitted
    elsewhere — so each chi factor's degrees of freedom live here, once, rather
    than being duplicated onto obligor rows.
    """

    factor_id: Sequence[int]
    factor_name: Sequence[str]
    factor_kind: Sequence[str]  # a CreditFactorKind name (SYSTEMIC, CHI, ...)


# ============================================================
# CREDIT RISK | DETERMINISTIC JTD
# ============================================================


class DeterministicJTDIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Deterministic JTD model frame.

    Carries only the dispatch keys (`uid`, `instrument`). LGD comes from
    the obligor master; PD is not read by the deterministic kernel; EAD
    flows in per scenario from the exposure engine. The frame is
    deliberately minimal — anything else here would duplicate state that
    already has a home elsewhere.
    """


# ============================================================
# CREDIT RISK | COPULA JTD (single-factor copulas)
# ============================================================


class _SingleFactorCopulaJTDCoreIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Single-factor copula JTD core: model-specific fields only.

    PD and LGD are joined off the obligor master at compile time and
    intentionally absent from this schema.
    """

    factor_loading: Sequence[float]
    systemic_factor: Sequence[str]
    idiosyncratic_factor: Sequence[str]


class GaussianCopulaJTDIngestData(_SingleFactorCopulaJTDCoreIngestData):
    pass


class TCopulaJTDIngestData(_SingleFactorCopulaJTDCoreIngestData):
    chi_squared_factor: Sequence[str]
    nu: Sequence[float]


# ============================================================
# CREDIT RISK | BERNOULLI-FAMILY JTD (conditional-independence)
# ============================================================


class IndependentBernoulliJTDIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Independent-Bernoulli JTD frame.

    Each obligor defaults if its OWN uniform draw falls below its (fixed,
    master-sourced) PD: ``u_i < pd_i``. The uniforms MUST be per-obligor
    distinct UNIFORM factors — sharing a row makes defaults comonotonic rather
    than independent. PD/LGD are joined off the obligor master.
    """

    uniform_factor: Sequence[str]


class StressRegimeBernoulliJTDIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Stress-regime Bernoulli JTD frame.

    Independent-Bernoulli with a single shared STRESS_INDICATOR factor that, when
    on, scales each obligor's PD by ``stress_pd_multiplier``. Uniforms are
    per-obligor distinct; the stress regime factor is global (shared).
    """

    uniform_factor: Sequence[str]
    stress_regime_factor: Sequence[str]
    stress_pd_multiplier: Sequence[float]


class BetaBinomialJTDIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Beta-binomial JTD frame.

    A shared per-scenario default probability ``P_s ~ Beta(alpha, beta)`` (drawn
    into the BETA_PD factor row) drives conditionally-independent obligor
    defaults ``u_i < P_s``. Uniforms are per-obligor distinct; the beta factor is
    shared (the common shock). PD is NOT carried — it is stochastic; only LGD is
    joined off the obligor master.
    """

    uniform_factor: Sequence[str]
    beta_factor: Sequence[str]


# ============================================================
# CREDIT RISK | MULTI-FACTOR COPULA JTD
# ============================================================


class MfGaussianCopulaJTDIngestData(_BaseIngestData):
    """
    Multi-factor Gaussian copula per-obligor frame: one row per obligor.

    The systemic loadings are NOT here — they are ragged (each obligor loads on
    a different subset of systemic factors), so they live in a separate
    long-format loadings frame (`MfCopulaLoadingsData`), one row per
    obligor x systemic factor, packed into CSR at compile time. Only the
    per-obligor idiosyncratic factor lives here. PD/LGD are joined off the
    obligor master.
    """

    idiosyncratic_factor: Sequence[str]


class MfCopulaLoadingsData(TypedDict):
    """
    Long-format multi-factor copula loadings: one row per obligor x systemic
    factor (`obligor_uid`, `factor_name`, `factor_loading`). Packed into CSR in
    block-band order at compile time; `sum factor_loading^2 <= 1` per obligor.
    """

    obligor_uid: Sequence[int]
    factor_name: Sequence[str]
    factor_loading: Sequence[float]
```

```python
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any, cast

from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.schemas import (
    BetaBinomialJTDIngestData,
    CreditFactorUniverseIngestData,
    DeterministicJTDIngestData,
    GaussianCopulaJTDIngestData,

    RiskStaticConfigCcy,

    RiskStaticConfigSpotRollRule,
    RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta,
    RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta,

    MarketRiskIngestData
    | GaussianCopulaJTDIngestData

    | BetaBinomialJTDIngestData

    # Risk Static Config

    | RiskStaticConfigSurfaceMeta
    | RiskStaticConfigSurfacePointMeta
    # Risk Proxy Config

)


def _validate_single_table(data: Mapping[str, Sequence[Any]]) -> None:
    lengths = {len(v) for v in data.values()}

    if len(lengths) > 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Inconsistent column lengths: {lengths}")


def validate_column_lengths(data: Any) -> None:
    """
    Validates:
    - flat columnar dicts: Dict[str, Sequence]
    - nested config: Dict[str, Dict[str, Sequence]]
    """

    # Case 1: Nested (RiskStaticConfigData)
    if all(isinstance(v, Mapping) for v in data.values()):
        for k, sub_table in data.items():
            if not isinstance(sub_table, Mapping):
                raise TypeError(f"{k} is not a valid table")

            _validate_single_table(cast(Mapping[str, Sequence[Any]], sub_table))
        return

    # Case 2: Flat table
    _validate_single_table(cast(Mapping[str, Sequence[Any]], data))


def generate_netting_set_ids(
    *,
    n: int,
    none_probability: float = 0.2,
) -> list[int | None]:

    import numpy as np

    from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.credit.netting_set.minimal_netting_set_master import (
        CANONICAL_NETTING_SET_UIDS,
    )
    from riskquant.ingest_fixtures.rng import get_canonical_rng

    _rng: np.random.Generator = get_canonical_rng()

    # 1. Generate the remaining IDs from valid options
    ns_ids = _rng.choice(
        a=CANONICAL_NETTING_SET_UIDS,
        size=n,
        replace=True,
    ).tolist()

    # 2. Randomly assign None based on the specified probability
    none_mask = _rng.random(size=n) < none_probability
    for i in range(n):
        if none_mask[i]:
            ns_ids[i] = None

    # 3. Combine and shuffle (shuffle works in-place)
    _rng.shuffle(ns_ids)

    return ns_ids
```

```python
# src/riskquant/market_data/assemble.py
"""
Market-data assembly: risk-factor levels -> `MarketDataMatrix`.

The producer-side tier directly above `market_data.builder`. Where
`builder.build_market_data_matrix` constructs a matrix from raw, typed,
universe-aligned arrays, this module assembles those arrays from a flat
`(N_req, S)` levels buffer (or a `RiskFactorLevelSnapshot`) using a
`UniverseMapping` slicing plan, then delegates down to the builder.

Layering
--------
This is the single, simulation-independent path from a levels snapshot to
a valuation-ready `MarketDataMatrix`. It lives in `market_data` precisely
so a plain valuation never has to import `simlab`: SIMLAB simulates the
levels; assembling a matrix from already-known levels is a market-data
concern. `simlab.runner.SimlabRunner` is one consumer; any base/t0
valuation is another.

`UniverseMapping` is the typed layout plan that extends the run's
`UixMapper` ID-universe into the dense, kernel-facing market-data axes.
`UixMapper` (built from the portfolio's ingest batches, now living in the
leaf module `riskquant.universe`) is the authority for the FX-effective
pair table, the date axis, and the market-/discount-id universes;
`UniverseMapping` adds the partition of those universes into the
`prices / forward_nodes / vol_nodes / fx_spots` scalar blocks plus the
curve/surface tenor grids, interp/extrap method codes, and surface y-axis
metadata sourced from `RiskStaticConfig`.

Scalar-node universe alignment (item C, option A1)
--------------------------------------------------
The compiled blocks index `prices` / `forward_nodes` / `vol_nodes` with a
`market_id_universe` UIX. So the dense scalar-node levels gathered here are
scattered by the builder into one `(len(market_id_universe), S)` matrix at
the universe positions carried by `scalar_node_uix_in_universe`; the three
pricer-facing fields alias that single matrix. The old behaviour (dense
`idx_*` slices handed straight to the matrix) was only correct when price
ids happened to occupy the front contiguous run of `market_id_universe`.

NOTE (carried forward): `UniverseMapping` currently straddles two
concerns. Every field except `required_factor_ids` / `required_shift_type_
ids` is pure market-data layout; those two are simlab shock-path inputs
(`extract_levels_from_snapshot` uses `required_factor_ids`; the shift-type
ids are consumed only by the runner's generator). A future split is
warranted — tracked in notes/REFACTORS_NEEDED.md.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date

import numpy as np
import numpy.typing as npt

from riskquant._arrays import searchsorted_or_raise
from riskquant.config.namespace import GlobalIdNamespace
from riskquant.config.static_config.static_config import RiskStaticConfig
from riskquant.market_data.builder import build_market_data_matrix
from riskquant.market_data.risk_factors.snapshot import RiskFactorLevelSnapshot
from riskquant.market_data.state import MarketDataMatrix
from riskquant.universe import UixMapper

# =========================================================================
# UNIVERSE MAPPING (TYPED LAYOUT PLAN)
# =========================================================================


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True, kw_only=True)
class UniverseMapping:
    """
    Slicing plan to extract the structured market-data arrays from one
    flat `(N_req, B)` levels matrix.

    The `idx_*` slices index the leading factor axis; reshape information
    for curves and surfaces lives in the `shape_*` triples.

    All `np.ndarray` fields hold per-config metadata pinned to the run's
    universe. Validation lives at build time; the runner trusts the
    contract.
    """

    required_factor_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int32]
    required_shift_type_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int8]

    # Scalars
    idx_fx_spots: slice

    # Scalar-node universe alignment (item C / A1)
    # --------------------------------------------
    # `idx_scalar_nodes` spans the scalar-node sub-block of `flat_levels`
    # (every NODE-topology rf in the run universe that is NOT an FX spot).
    # `scalar_node_uix_in_universe` is the `market_id_universe` row for each
    # of those dense rows, so the builder scatters them into the universe-
    # aligned `(len(market_id_universe), S)` scalar matrix. Under A1
    # `prices`/`forward_nodes`/`vol_nodes` alias that one matrix, so there is
    # no per-class (price / fwd / vol) storage partition: those distinctions
    # are pricer-side consumption, not a layout concern, and were removed
    # (S1). A node's economic kind is a config property; its level lives at
    # its universe row regardless of which block reads it.
    idx_scalar_nodes: slice
    scalar_node_uix_in_universe: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    # Curves / surfaces
    idx_discount_curves: slice
    shape_discount_curves: tuple[int, int]  # (C_curves, M_tenors)

    idx_price_curves: slice
    shape_price_curves: tuple[int, int]

    # Price-curve universe alignment (item C / A1, curve layer)
    # ---------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward-curve underlyings resolve to a curve CONTAINER id that lands
    # in `market_id_universe` (`compile_vanilla_forward_curve` sets
    # `price_curve_uix = map_market_ids_to_uix(...)`), and the kernel reads
    # `price_curve_grid[price_curve_uix, delivery_uix, s]`. So the builder
    # must scatter the dense `(C, D, S)` interpolated grid into a
    # `(len(market_id_universe), D, S)` array at these universe rows — the
    # exact A1 treatment, one axis up. `price_curve_uix_in_universe[c]` is
    # the `market_id_universe` row of the c-th price-curve container (same
    # order as the `idx_price_curves` curve blocks). Empty when the run
    # references no forward curves.
    price_curve_uix_in_universe: npt.NDArray[np.int32]

    idx_vol_surfaces: slice
    shape_vol_surfaces: tuple[int, int, int]  # (C_surfaces, M_tenors, K_strikes)

    # 1-D curve configs
    curve_interp_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8]
    curve_extrap_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8]
    raw_discount_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64]
    raw_price_curve_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64]

    # 2-D surface configs
    surface_interp_x_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8]
    surface_extrap_x_methods: npt.NDArray[np.int8]
    surface_y_axis_type_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int8]
    raw_vol_surface_tenors: npt.NDArray[np.float64]
    raw_vol_surface_strikes: npt.NDArray[np.float64]

    # Container ids for each row of the surface batch — length C_surfaces,
    # int32. Lets builder-side consumers map a `c` row index in the
    # surface arrays back to its `market_id_universe` slot via
    # `np.searchsorted(uix_mapper.market_id_universe, surface_ids)`.
    # Required for any future scatter-into-universe pass (e.g. surface-
    # ATM synthesis of `vol_nodes` slots, market-id-universe-aligned
    # surface metadata propagation). Defaulted to an empty array so
    # universes that declare zero surfaces remain valid.
    surface_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int32] = field(
        default_factory=lambda: np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32),
    )


# =========================================================================
# UNIVERSE MAPPING BUILDER (config SSOT -> run-scoped layout plan)
# =========================================================================


def _classify_run_universe(
    *,
    market_id_universe: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    static_config: RiskStaticConfig,
) -> tuple[
    npt.NDArray[np.int32],  # scalar-RF ids   (node OR curve/surface POINT)
    npt.NDArray[np.int32],  # fx-spot rf ids  (subset of scalar)
    npt.NDArray[np.int32],  # price-curve CONTAINER ids
    npt.NDArray[np.int32],  # vol-surface CONTAINER ids
]:
    """
    Partition the run's `market_id_universe` by GlobalIdNamespace RANGE.

    Contract (pinned):
      - `< 100M`  (NODE range): a SCALAR risk factor an instrument prices off
        as a single value. This is EITHER a standalone node (node_meta) OR a
        curve/surface POINT (a pillar rf an instrument references directly).
        Both are read as one scalar level and both scatter into the A1
        `scalar_levels` matrix at their `market_id_universe` row. The point-
        vs-node topology distinction is irrelevant to the scalar read path,
        so we deliberately do NOT branch on `rf_id_to_parent_topology_type`
        here — range is the role boundary.
      - `[100M, 200M)` (CURVE range): a price-curve CONTAINER an instrument
        interpolates off. Routed to its own grid universe.
      - `[200M, 300M)` (SURFACE range): a vol-surface CONTAINER.

    FX spots are the scalar subset whose id is in `fx_spot_lookup`'s values.

    Membership comes from the validated run universe; the split is pure
    `GlobalIdNamespace` arithmetic — nothing inferred from the portfolio.
    """
    ids = market_id_universe
    is_node_range = (ids >= GlobalIdNamespace.NODES.min_id) & (
        ids <= GlobalIdNamespace.NODES.max_id
    )
    is_curve_range = (ids >= GlobalIdNamespace.CURVES.min_id) & (
        ids <= GlobalIdNamespace.CURVES.max_id
    )
    is_surface_range = (ids >= GlobalIdNamespace.SURFACES.min_id) & (
        ids <= GlobalIdNamespace.SURFACES.max_id
    )

    out_of_range = ~(is_node_range | is_curve_range | is_surface_range)
    if out_of_range.any():
        raise ValueError(
            "market_id_universe contains ids outside every GlobalIdNamespace "
            f"block: {ids[out_of_range][:10].tolist()}."
        )

    fx_spot_ids = frozenset(int(v) for v in static_config.fx_spot_lookup.values())
    is_fx = np.fromiter(
        (int(i) in fx_spot_ids for i in ids),
        dtype=np.bool_,
        count=ids.shape[0],
    )

    scalar_rfs = ids[is_node_range & ~is_fx]
    fx_spots = ids[is_node_range & is_fx]
    price_curve_containers = ids[is_curve_range]
    vol_surface_containers = ids[is_surface_range]
    return scalar_rfs, fx_spots, price_curve_containers, vol_surface_containers


def build_universe_mapping(
    *,
    static_config: RiskStaticConfig,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
    vol_surface_ids: Sequence[int] = (),
) -> UniverseMapping:
    """
    Build the run-scoped market-data layout plan from config SSOT.

    The run universe (`uix_mapper.market_id_universe` + the discount uix
    universe) decides WHICH factors are laid out; `RiskStaticConfig` decides
    WHAT each one is. Membership is the validated subset the portfolio
    references; classification is pure config lookup. Nothing is inferred
    from the portfolio.

    Price (forward) curves are NOT passed in — a forward-curve underlying
    resolves to a curve CONTAINER id that lands in `market_id_universe`
    (`compile_vanilla_forward_curve` -> `map_market_ids_to_uix`), so the
    CURVE-topology members of `market_id_universe` ARE the run's price
    curves. Discount curves come from `discount_curve_id_universe`.

    Layout / `required_factor_ids` order (the `flat_levels` row order the
    `idx_*` slices then re-slice):

        [ fx_spots | scalar_nodes | discount_curve_pts | price_curve_pts ]

    Curve points are emitted curve-by-curve in `curve_id_to_tenor_index`
    offset order so the `(C, M)` reshape in the builder is exact.

    Universe alignment (item C / A1): `scalar_node_uix_in_universe` and
    `price_curve_uix_in_universe` carry each scalar node's / price-curve
    container's `market_id_universe` row, so the builder scatters both the
    dense scalar block and the dense `(C, D, S)` curve grid into universe-
    aligned arrays.

    Raises
    ------
    NotImplementedError
        If the run references vol surfaces (any SURFACE-topology member of
        `market_id_universe`, or a non-empty `vol_surface_ids`). The surface
        `(C, M, K)` grid assembly from raw `surface_point_meta` has no
        reference producer to verify against and is intentionally NOT
        fabricated blind — see notes/REFACTORS_NEEDED.md (P2 follow-up).
        Scalar nodes, FX spots, discount curves, and price (forward) curves
        are fully supported.
    """
    mkt_uni = uix_mapper.market_id_universe
    disc_uni = uix_mapper.discount_curve_id_universe

    scalar_rfs, fx_spots, price_curve_ids, surface_ids = _classify_run_universe(
        market_id_universe=mkt_uni, static_config=static_config
    )
    if surface_ids.size or vol_surface_ids:
        raise NotImplementedError(
            "build_universe_mapping: vol-surface layout is not yet implemented "
            "(the (C, M, K) grid assembly from surface_point_meta has no "
            f"reference producer to verify against). Surface containers in run: "
            f"{surface_ids[:5].tolist()}. Scalar nodes, FX spots, discount "
            "curves, and price curves are supported. See REFACTORS_NEEDED.md."
        )

    interp = static_config.container_interp_x_id
    extrap = static_config.container_extrap_x_id
    tenor_index = static_config.curve_id_to_tenor_index

    # ---- Curve point layout: discount then price, curve-by-curve --------
    def _curve_block(
        container_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    ) -> tuple[list[int], list[float], list[int], list[int], int]:
        child_ids: list[int] = []
        tenors: list[float] = []
        interps: list[int] = []
        extraps: list[int] = []
        m_ref: int | None = None
        for cid in container_ids.tolist():
            ti = tenor_index[int(cid)]
            offs = ti.sorted_offsets
            ids = ti.rf_ids_by_offset
            if m_ref is None:
                m_ref = offs.shape[0]
            elif offs.shape[0] != m_ref:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"curve {cid} has {offs.shape[0]} tenors but the batch "
                    f"expects a rectangular {m_ref}; ragged curve grids are "
                    "not supported in one batch."
                )
            child_ids.extend(int(x) for x in ids.tolist())
            tenors.extend(float(x) for x in offs.tolist())
            cslot = static_config.container_slot(int(cid))
            interps.append(int(interp[cslot]))
            extraps.append(int(extrap[cslot]))
        return child_ids, tenors, interps, extraps, (m_ref or 0)

    disc_ids = np.asarray(disc_uni, dtype=np.int32)
    price_ids = np.asarray(price_curve_ids, dtype=np.int32)  # CURVE members of mkt_uni

    disc_children, disc_tenors, _disc_interp, _disc_extrap, disc_m = _curve_block(
        disc_ids
    )
    price_children, price_tenors, price_interp, price_extrap, price_m = _curve_block(
        price_ids
    )

    # ---- Assemble required_factor_ids + idx_* in one pass ---------------
    n_fx = fx_spots.shape[0]
    n_scalar = scalar_rfs.shape[0]
    n_disc = len(disc_children)
    n_price = len(price_children)

    required = np.concatenate(
        [
            fx_spots,
            scalar_rfs,
            np.asarray(disc_children, dtype=np.int32),
            np.asarray(price_children, dtype=np.int32),
        ]
    ).astype(np.int32, copy=False)

    off = 0
    idx_fx = slice(off, off + n_fx)
    off += n_fx
    idx_scalar = slice(off, off + n_scalar)
    off += n_scalar
    idx_disc = slice(off, off + n_disc)
    off += n_disc
    idx_price = slice(off, off + n_price)
    off += n_price

    # Scalar-RF + price-curve universe rows (A1 scatter targets).
    scalar_uix = np.searchsorted(mkt_uni, scalar_rfs).astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    price_curve_uix = np.searchsorted(mkt_uni, price_ids).astype(np.int32, copy=False)

    required_shift = static_config.rf_id_to_shift_type_id[required].astype(
        np.int8, copy=False
    )

    return UniverseMapping(
        required_factor_ids=required,
        required_shift_type_ids=required_shift,
        idx_fx_spots=idx_fx,
        idx_scalar_nodes=idx_scalar,
        scalar_node_uix_in_universe=scalar_uix,
        idx_discount_curves=idx_disc,
        shape_discount_curves=(disc_ids.shape[0], disc_m),
        idx_price_curves=idx_price,
        shape_price_curves=(price_ids.shape[0], price_m),
        price_curve_uix_in_universe=price_curve_uix,
        idx_vol_surfaces=slice(off, off),
        shape_vol_surfaces=(0, 0, 0),
        # Discount curves use a hardwired log-linear kernel and ignore method
        # codes; `curve_interp/extrap_methods` feed ONLY the price-curve grid
        # builder, so they carry price-curve entries exclusively (length
        # C_price). `disc_interp`/`disc_extrap` are computed but unused.
        curve_interp_methods=np.asarray(price_interp, dtype=np.int8),
        curve_extrap_methods=np.asarray(price_extrap, dtype=np.int8),
        raw_discount_tenors=np.asarray(disc_tenors, dtype=np.float64).reshape(
            disc_ids.shape[0], disc_m
        ),
        raw_price_curve_tenors=np.asarray(price_tenors, dtype=np.float64).reshape(
            price_ids.shape[0], price_m
        ),
        surface_interp_x_methods=np.empty(0, dtype=np.int8),
        surface_extrap_x_methods=np.empty(0, dtype=np.int8),
        surface_y_axis_type_ids=np.empty(0, dtype=np.int8),
        raw_vol_surface_tenors=np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64),
        raw_vol_surface_strikes=np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64),
        surface_ids=np.empty(0, dtype=np.int32),
    )


# =========================================================================
# LEVELS GATHER
# =========================================================================


def extract_levels_from_snapshot(
    *,
    required_ids: npt.NDArray[np.int32],
    snapshot: RiskFactorLevelSnapshot,
) -> npt.NDArray[np.float64]:
    """
    Vectorised O(N log K) gather of base levels by required-id.

    `snapshot.factor_ids` is sorted ascending (the Snapshot class
    invariant), enabling one-shot `searchsorted` membership check
    and gather.
    """
    idx = searchsorted_or_raise(
        universe=snapshot.factor_ids,
        raw=required_ids,
        label="risk factors required by the compiled portfolio but missing "
        "from the snapshot",
    )
    return np.ascontiguousarray(snapshot.factor_levels[idx]).astype(
        np.float64, copy=False
    )


# =========================================================================
# MATRIX ASSEMBLY
# =========================================================================


def build_market_data_matrix_from_flat_levels(
    *,
    as_of_date: date,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
    universe_map: UniverseMapping,
    flat_levels: npt.NDArray[np.float64],
    num_scenarios: int,
) -> MarketDataMatrix:
    """
    Build a `MarketDataMatrix` from a flat `(N_req, S)` levels matrix.

    Slicing produces views, not copies. The builder owns its own
    per-build allocations (FX-effective gather, curve grids).
    """
    u = universe_map

    raw_fx_spots = flat_levels[u.idx_fx_spots, :]
    raw_scalar_nodes = flat_levels[u.idx_scalar_nodes, :]

    raw_discount_ln_dfs = flat_levels[u.idx_discount_curves, :].reshape(
        u.shape_discount_curves[0],
        u.shape_discount_curves[1],
        num_scenarios,
    )
    raw_price_curve_values = flat_levels[u.idx_price_curves, :].reshape(
        u.shape_price_curves[0],
        u.shape_price_curves[1],
        num_scenarios,
    )

    raw_vol_surface_values = flat_levels[u.idx_vol_surfaces, :].reshape(
        u.shape_vol_surfaces[0],
        u.shape_vol_surfaces[1],
        u.shape_vol_surfaces[2],
        num_scenarios,
    )

    return build_market_data_matrix(
        as_of_date=as_of_date,
        uix_mapper=uix_mapper,
        num_scenarios=num_scenarios,
        raw_fx_spots=raw_fx_spots,
        raw_scalar_nodes=raw_scalar_nodes,
        scalar_node_uix_in_universe=u.scalar_node_uix_in_universe,
        raw_discount_tenors=u.raw_discount_tenors,
        raw_discount_ln_dfs=raw_discount_ln_dfs,
        raw_price_curve_tenors=u.raw_price_curve_tenors,
        raw_price_curve_values=raw_price_curve_values,
        curve_interp_methods=u.curve_interp_methods,
        curve_extrap_methods=u.curve_extrap_methods,
        price_curve_uix_in_universe=u.price_curve_uix_in_universe,
        raw_vol_surface_tenors=u.raw_vol_surface_tenors,
        raw_vol_surface_strikes=u.raw_vol_surface_strikes,
        raw_vol_surface_values=raw_vol_surface_values,
        surface_interp_x_methods=u.surface_interp_x_methods,
        surface_extrap_x_methods=u.surface_extrap_x_methods,
        surface_y_axis_type_ids=u.surface_y_axis_type_ids,
    )


def build_market_data_matrix_from_snapshot(
    *,
    as_of_date: date,
    uix_mapper: UixMapper,
    universe_map: UniverseMapping,
    snapshot: RiskFactorLevelSnapshot,
    num_scenarios: int = 1,
) -> MarketDataMatrix:
    """
    Build a `MarketDataMatrix` directly from a `RiskFactorLevelSnapshot`.
    """
    base_levels = extract_levels_from_snapshot(
        required_ids=universe_map.required_factor_ids,
        snapshot=snapshot,
    )
    if num_scenarios == 1:
        flat_levels = base_levels[:, None]
    else:
        flat_levels = np.broadcast_to(
            base_levels[:, None], (base_levels.shape[0], num_scenarios)
        )
    return build_market_data_matrix_from_flat_levels(
        as_of_date=as_of_date,
        uix_mapper=uix_mapper,
        universe_map=universe_map,
        flat_levels=flat_levels,
        num_scenarios=num_scenarios,
    )
```
</file>
